Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in numerical_analysis theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 775 and the invariant I(A,B) = 369 mod 2.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 775 + O(1/n^369). Summing over all modes and applying the numerical_analysis convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

Theorem 220.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For any numerical_analysis module M satisfying the structural decomposition hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 94. The projective resolution has length at most 980 with Betti numbers bounded by 3.

Proof: We apply the structural decomposition principle to the numerical_analysis decomposition. By minimality, the element a^94 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 980-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
